Flexible Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)

‚öñÔ∏è Separation of Duties Guardrails & Governance Policy

Configure automated backend restrictions to enforce dual sign-off (Four-Eyes Principle) and prevent single reviewers from performing conflicting approval actions.

1. Four-Eyes Principle (Dual Sign-Off)
The staff member who performs the initial KYC/KYB document review CANNOT execute the final approval decision. Requires second reviewer.
2. Prevent Self-Approval on Escalations
A reviewer cannot approve a case they previously escalated. Escalated cases must be resolved by another authorized staff member.
3. Prevent Self-Moderation Decision Overrides
A review moderator cannot modify or override their own previous moderation decision without formal escalation.
4. High-Risk Cases Require Super Admin Approval
Cases flagged with High Risk (e.g. multiple TIN registrations or defamation claims) strictly require signoff from a Super Admin.

Staff SOPs, Rubrics & Investigation Notes

üìå Core Staff Verification & Review Rubrics

1. DTI & BIR Merchant Validation Checklist
  • Verify SEC/DTI Certificate number against government registry database.
  • Match BIR 2303 TIN with authorized signatory ID.
  • Confirm physical business address on Google Maps StreetView if flagged.
2. Escrow Milestone Dispute Protocol
  • Check milestone deliverables against agreed project contract scope.
  • Request proof of communication (chat logs, file attachments) within 48h.
  • Release partial funds if separable work items are completed satisfactorily.
3. AI Fake Review & Astroturfing Policy
  • Gemini 3.7 moderation score < 40 requires manual reviewer cross-check.
  • Check for suspicious bursts of 5-star or 1-star reviews from same subnet.
  • Ensure verified receipt proof is attached for disputed complaints.
